### Information: 
seat earnings per league per seat:
4. League: VIP: 212; sit: 16; stand: 8
3. League: VIP: 269; sit: 21; stand: 10
2. League: VIP: 343; sit: 27; stand: 13
1. League: VIP: 436, sit: 34; stand: 17

max seats per league: 
4. League: VIP: 1500; sit: 20000
3. League: VIP: 1900; sit: 24000
2. League: VIP: 2300; sit: 28500
1. League: VIP: 2800; sit: 35000

You can build an unlimited number of standing seats. But not all will be filled. The maximum number depends on the League and how the team is doing. So if the team wins often or looses often. 

cost to build seats: 
Stand: 10k per 100 seats
Sit: 30k per 100 seats
VIP: 20k per 100 seats

Duration to build seats: 
Stand: 1h40min per 100 seats
Sit: 2h per 100 seats
VIP. 50 min per 10 seats

### Idea why it fails: 
I believe it may be because in the API response the number of seats is not divisable by 100 for the standing and sitting seats. When I try to build more VIP seats the game doesn't crash and it seems to work. But it only goes in steps of 210 seats. Not 10. 
The running const and earnings are swapped, that isn't a reason that it fails, but it needs to be fixed. 
To fix this compare it carefully to captured output under information/original_API_requests/ and work the format out. Compare that to the things the app expects and write an analysis in information/api_analysis/. Then fix it.

### Information: 
The filter options are: 
"Strength": {
    "Min": 33,
    "Max": 500
  },
  "Age": {
    "Min": 28,
    "Max": 34
  },
  "Talent": {
    "Min": 3,
    "Max": 10
  }
Additionally you can filter by position. If there is no filter for position set it sends: 
"SkillIndex": -1,
For defender it doesn't send "SkippIndex". For keeper 1, for midfielder 2 and for striker 3. When a position is chosen a "Skill": {"Min": 22,"Max": 500} may be send. That is for the skill in the positions main skill, for defenders defence for example. Another option for filter is "Budget": 9985300.0. For that the bidding price has to be under that budget. 

### Idea why it fails: 
I believe the search doesn't work because the server doesn't support it. For the other, no idea. Compare the request and response to the information the app expects and compare it to the collected API requests and responses under information/original_API_requests/. Write an analysis in information/api_analysis/ and then fix it.

## Friends: 
The search for accounts doesn't work. 

### Idea why it fails: 
When "Text": {search string} is sent with on https://gt.nikolai-linschmann.de/api/Friends/GetFriends it should show accounts that include this search pattern. Not only friends. "Language": "de" for example can be send too as an additional search filter. That should show all the teams from that language group. Write an analysis in information/api_analysis/ and then fix it.
